### [1] [What Improves Multimodal Misinformation Detection? Answers from a Large-Scale Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30402)
*Akshit Sharma,Prashant W. Patil*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对多模态虚假信息检测中的设计选择进行了大规模实证研究，通过375+个实验、三个基准数据集及多种预训练视觉/语言骨干网络，系统比较并解析各设计选择的效果、失效情境及对模型行为的塑造作用，回答了4个关键研究问题，为构建更强壮、更可靠的多模态虚假信息检测系统提供了实践指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态虚假信息常通过"文本声明+佐证图像"的组合来伪装成可信内容，然而实际构建高效检测器时，往往高度依赖少量设计选择，这些选择很少在受控条件下被系统检验。作者旨在填补这一空白，提供关于设计选择何时有效、何时悄然失效的经验性证据，为研究社区奠定可靠的设计基础。

Method: 作者开展大规模受控研究：在三个基准数据集上，结合广泛的预训练视觉与语言骨干网络，进行了超过3,375次实验；通过系统性对比和针对性鲁棒性分析，评估多模态设计选择的影响，并据此归纳实践指导，回答4个专项研究问题（RQs）。

Result: 研究提炼出关于"哪些设计选择有帮助、哪些情况下会无声失效、以及流程中哪些环节最能塑造模型行为"的实践指导；系统回答了4个关键研究问题，明确了影响多模态虚假信息检测性能的关键设计因素。

Conclusion: 该研究为设计更强壮、更可靠的多模态虚假信息检测系统提供了可靠的经验基础，其系统性的实证发现可为更广泛的研究社区、后续方法开发与评估提供参考与指导。

Abstract: Multimodal misinformation is increasingly crafted to look convincing by pairing a textual claim with an image that appears to "prove" it. Yet in practice, building effective detectors often hinges on a small set of design choices that are rarely examined in a controlled way. In this paper, we conduct a large-scale study of multimodal design choices for misinformation detection with over 3,375 experiments- spanning three benchmark datasets and a broad range of pre-trained vision and language backbones. Through systematic comparisons and targeted robustness analyses, we distill practical guidance on which design choices help, when do they fail silently, and what aspects of the pipeline most strongly shape model behavior, answering 4 key Research Questions (RQs). We aim to provide a reliable foundation for designing stronger and more dependable multimodal misinformation detection systems, thus contributing to the broader research community.

</details>


### [2] [Action Forcing: Training World Models on Unsupervised Video by Recovering Underlying Egomotion Bases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30595)
*Ashish Sundar,Tiankuo Hou,Zhong Fan,Chunbo Luo,Xiaoyang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需人工标注或传感器校准的、从普通未标注视频中提取可泛化的动作标注（action annotations）的方法，用于训练可控世界模型。利用PCA分解像素位移得到数据驱动的自运动基（egomotion basis），通过在线潜在评论家（latent critic）将冻结的decoder-tracker-PCA教师模型蒸馏到视频DiT中，获得有grounding的油门-偏航控制信号。同时引入参考无关的评估指标（可控性、合理性、无中生有、几何完整性），揭示传统视频生成指标无法发现的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可控世界模型需要同步的动作标注来训练，但现有数据集要么依赖带校准传感器的仪器平台，要么依赖昂贵的人工标注，要么使用缺乏grounding的潜在动作模型。这些方法成本高且难以扩展，需要一种从普通视频中低成本获取grounded动作信号的方法。

Method: (1) 从像素级位移跟踪中提取跨帧位移，利用PCA提取数据驱动的自运动基，得到有符号、可缩放、可组合的油门-偏航控制；(2) 使用在线潜在评论家（online latent critic）蒸馏冻结的decoder-tracker-PCA教师模型，避免对高容量视频DiT在像素级监督上的过拟合，且不通过decoder或tracker反向传播；(3) 引入参考无关的评估方法，衡量可控性、合理性、conjuring（对象凭空出现）和几何完整性。

Result: 多数基线模型能跟随熟悉的动作方向但难以逆转动作或保持静止；本文模型既能处理反向动作（尽管训练数据中反向动作占比不足1%），又能线性缩放响应、组合油门与转向，同时保持构图控制能力和生成质量。新评估指标揭示了传统视频生成指标忽略的失败模式。

Conclusion: 通过grounded动作空间进行学习，即使训练数据中某种动作模式极为稀少，模型也能学会反转、线性缩放和组合控制，表明数据驱动的PCA基方法可以作为动作监督的有效替代方案，并且参考无关评估可以弥补传统视频生成指标在评估世界模型方面的不足。

Abstract: Synchronised action annotations are needed to train controllable world models and these datasets remain elusive. Existing approaches make use of instrumented platforms with calibrated sensors, costly manual annotation, or latent-action models which lack grounding. We instead turn ordinary unlabelled video into action-supervised training data by recovering (without training) a data-derived egomotion basis. We track pixel displacements across frames and exploit the recurring coherent structure induced by egomotion to obtain grounded control signals directly. Using a method as simple as principal components analysis perform this, we find that the leading components provide signed, scalable, and composable throttle--yaw controls, although the method can recover only motion axes represented in the data. To prevent a high-capacity video DiT from exploiting pixel-level supervision, an online latent critic distils a frozen decoder--tracker--PCA (Principal Components Analysis) teacher without backpropagating through the decoder or tracker. Finally we critique the use of video generation metrics to evaluate WMs and introduce an example of an alternative, reference-free evaluation method. We measure \textit{controllability}, \textit{plausibility}, \textit{conjuring} (creating objects out of thin air) and \textit{geometric integrity}, revealing failures that conventional video metrics miss. We show that most baselines follow familiar action directions but struggle to reverse or remain stationary. Our model handles both while retaining compositional control and generation quality. Despite backwards actions being less than $1\%$ of our training data, we find that the model learns to reverse, scale its response linearly, and compose throttle with steering, all simply by learning through a grounded action space.

</details>


### [3] [MedTokenBudget: Lesion-Preserving Token Routing for Dermoscopic Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30613)
*Zhexiang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 MedTokenBudget，一个基于 ViT 的监督式 token 路由框架，在辅助病灶掩码可用时，通过 Lesion-Aware Token Scoring (LATS) 模块学习构建紧凑且富含病灶信息的表征，在预算约束下保留 top-K 补丁，并在 ISIC 2019 上优于 Random 和 ToMe 基线，保留更多病灶补丁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 皮肤镜分类器中的 ViT 对所有图像补丁一视同仁地处理，但诊断证据主要集中于病灶区域。现有的 token 剪枝方法使用通用显著性或相似性信号来减少 token，却很少验证保留的子集是否仍包含病灶，导致可能丢失关键诊断信息。本文旨在解决这一问题。

Method: MedTokenBudget 是一个监督式后骨干（post-backbone）token 路由框架。其核心 Lesion-Aware Token Scoring (LATS) 模块通过一个可学习评分器融合注意力熵、特征范数和局部特征对比度三个信号，在目标预算下路由 top-K 补丁。LATS 通过预算课程学习、多样性正则化、注意力蒸馏和病灶掩码监督进行联合训练，并以病灶保留率（lesion retention rate）直接衡量预算下保留的地面真值病灶证据比例。

Result: 在 ISIC 2019 数据集上，掩码监督的 LATS 在主要预算下持续优于 Random 和 ToMe 基线，同时保留显著更多的病灶补丁；代码已提供以保证可复现性，完整表格结果在补充材料中给出。

Conclusion: 通过病灶掩码监督与多信号融合的可学习评分，监督式 token 路由能够在预算受限时构造病灶富集的紧凑表征，相较于通用剪枝方法显著改善病灶证据的保留，验证了结合领域先验（病灶掩码）进行 token 剪枝的有效性。

Abstract: Dermoscopy classifiers built on Vision Transformers process all image patches uniformly, although diagnostic evidence is concentrated in the lesion region. Existing token pruning methods reduce tokens using generic saliency or similarity signals, but rarely ask whether the retained subset still contains the lesion. This paper introduces MedTokenBudget, a supervised post-backbone token routing framework that learns to construct compact lesion-enriched representations when auxiliary lesion masks are available. Its Lesion-Aware Token Scoring (LATS) module fuses attention entropy, feature norm, and local feature contrast through a learned scorer, then routes the top-$K$ patches under a target budget. LATS is trained with budget curriculum learning, diversity regularization, attention distillation, and lesion-mask supervision. The trained router is evaluated with a lesion retention rate that directly measures how much ground-truth lesion evidence survives the token budget. On ISIC 2019, mask-supervised LATS consistently outperforms Random and ToMe at headline budgets while retaining substantially more lesion patches. Code is provided for reproducibility, and complete tabulated results are included in the supplementary material.

</details>


### [4] [SAGE: Source-Anchored Guidance via Frequency Equalization for Hierarchical RGB-T Alignment and Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30703)
*Timing Li,Yiming Sun,Boan Tao,Xiyuan Gao,Haifang Cao,Pengfei Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SAGE框架，一个统一的RGB-T融合系统，通过频率均衡、层次化对齐和子带融合，解决空间失配和跨模态差异导致的鬼影、结构模糊和内容失衡问题，在真实和合成错位数据集上取得竞争性性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RGB-T融合方法将外观适应、几何对齐和信息融合解耦为独立阶段，限制了阶段间的依赖传播，导致在弱配准输入下出现鬼影、结构模糊和内容失衡等问题。

Method: SAGE采用可逆联合编码和源特定低频调制生成结构与增益引导；通过层次化频率协同对齐，利用低频近似估计全局仿射几何，并将几何和上下文线索传递至高频相关性推理进行可靠性感知残差细化；引导子带融合在对齐的频率系数上联合聚合，协调高低频信息，最终通过逆小波变换重建融合图像。

Result: 在具有真实和合成错位的RGB-T数据集上，SAGE在对齐和融合任务中均取得一致的竞争性性能，验证了源锚定引导对弱配准RGB-T图像的有效性。

Conclusion: SAGE通过统一框架实现了频率均衡、层次化对齐与子带融合的一体化设计，有效解决了弱配准RGB-T图像的融合难题，证实了源锚定引导策略的优越性。

Abstract: Spatial misregistration and cross-modal discrepancies often cause ghosting, structural blurring, and content imbalance in RGB-T fusion. Existing methods typically decouple appearance adaptation, geometric alignment, and information fusion, limiting dependency propagation across stages. We propose Source-Anchored Guidance via Frequency Equalization for Hierarchical RGB-T Alignment and Fusion (SAGE), a unified framework integrating frequency equalization, hierarchical alignment, and subband fusion. SAGE employs invertible joint encoding and source-specific low-frequency modulation to derive structural and gain guidance while preserving source information. Hierarchical frequency collaborative alignment estimates global affine geometry from low-frequency approximations and transfers geometric and contextual cues to high-frequency correlation reasoning for reliability-aware residual refinement. Guided subband fusion jointly aggregates the aligned frequency coefficients under propagated source and alignment guidance, coordinates complementary low- and high-frequency information, and reconstructs the fused image through the inverse wavelet transform. Extensive experiments on RGB-T datasets with real-world and synthetic misalignments demonstrate consistently competitive performance in alignment and fusion, validating the effectiveness of source-anchored guidance for weakly registered RGB-T images.

</details>


### [5] [Combining General and Domain-Specific Pretext Tasks for Brain MR Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30708)
*Tasneem Nasser,Susanne Schmid,Roberto Souza,Naser El-Sheimy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多任务自监督预训练框架，将体素级脑龄预测（领域特定任务）与图像修复（通用任务）联合优化，以学习互补的神经影像表征。预训练模型在三个下游MRI分割任务（多发性硬化病灶、缺血性卒中病灶、皮层脑结构分割）上评估，结果表明所提出的多任务框架在大多数实验设置中优于单任务预训练和从头训练，验证了结合领域特定与通用自监督任务对构建神经影像基础模型的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像分析面临特定人群和疾病的大规模标注数据集稀缺的挑战。由于深度学习模型高度依赖标注数据，需要有效的迁移学习策略来减少对手动标注的依赖。自监督学习是开发基础模型的有前景途径，但需要探索更适合神经影像领域的预训练任务设计。

Method: 将体素级脑龄预测作为领域特定的自监督预文本任务，与广泛使用的非领域特定任务（图像修复）进行比较；进而提出多任务自监督预训练框架，联合优化这两个目标以学习互补的神经影像表征。

Result: 预训练模型在三个下游MRI分割任务（多发性硬化病灶分割、缺血性卒中病灶分割、皮层脑结构分割）上评估。所提出的多任务预训练框架在大多数实验设置中一致优于单任务预训练模型和从头训练的模型。

Conclusion: 结合领域特定与通用自监督学习预文本任务可构建更可泛化的神经影像基础模型，证明了多任务自监督预训练框架在医学图像分析中的有效性。

Abstract: A key challenge in medical image analysis is the scarcity of large annotated datasets for specific populations and diseases. As deep learning models rely heavily on labeled data, effective transfer learning strategies are needed to reduce the dependence on manual annotations. Self-supervised learning has emerged as a promising approach for developing foundation models by enabling the learning of transferable feature representations from large-scale unlabeled medical imaging datasets. In this study, we investigate voxel-level brain age prediction as a domain-specific self-supervised pretext task and compare it with image inpainting, a widely used non-domain-specific alternative. We further propose a multitask self-supervised pretraining framework that jointly optimizes both objectives to learn complementary neuroimaging representations. The pretrained models are evaluated on three downstream magnetic resonance image segmentation tasks: multiple sclerosis lesion segmentation, ischemic stroke lesion segmentation, and cortical brain structure segmentation. Overall, the proposed multitask pretraining framework consistently outperformed the single-task pretrained models and training from scratch across most experimental settings, demonstrating the benefit of combining domain-specific and general self-supervised learning pretext tasks for the development of generalizable neuroimaging foundation models.\ Code Availability: The source code used in this study is publicly available at https://github.com/TasneemN/Combining-General-and-Domain-Specific-Pretext-Tasks-for-Brain-MR-Image-Segmentation/

</details>


### [6] [VLALight: Lightweight Vision-Language-Action Models for Emergency-Aware Traffic Signal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30709)
*Kemou Jiang,Maonan Wang,Xingchen Zou,Jiayue Zhu,Yuhang Fu,Sicheng Wang,Xi Chen,Yirong Chen,Zhiyong Cui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VLALight 是一个轻量级端到端视觉-语言-动作（vision-language-action）框架，直接将路口观测与信号相位信息映射为离散信号动作，使用 0.5B 参数的紧凑模型，无需中间图像到文本转换或手工交通状态表示，在实时运行的同时将应急车辆等待时间降低 21.1%，并能泛化到未见过的路口拓扑与交通流模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来视觉语言模型（VLM）为交通信号控制（TSC）提供了更丰富的路口场景理解能力，但现有方法在模块间采用松耦合与反复的信息转换，导致细粒度视觉细节丢失，且顺序推理带来显著延迟。作者旨在解决这些问题。

Method: 提出 VLALight 端到端框架：将多方向摄像头视图合并为统一视觉输入，利用文本指令建立其与交通流向及信号相位的对应关系，从而以 0.5B 参数模型直接预测动作，避免中间图像描述与手工交通状态表示。

Result: 实验表明 VLALight 在应急车辆服务方面优于所有对比方法，相较于级联式 VLMLight 将综合应急等待时间降低 21.1%，且能在本地硬件上实时运行，并可泛化到未见路口拓扑与交通流模式。

Conclusion: VLALight 通过紧凑的端到端设计与多视图信息融合，实现了低延迟、高实时性的视觉上下文感知交通信号控制，同时兼顾应急响应与泛化能力，为实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Traffic signal control (TSC) is essential for mitigating urban congestion. Recent advances in vision-language models (VLMs) enable richer interpretation of intersection scenes, opening new opportunities for visual-context-aware TSC. However, the loose coupling and repeated information conversion between modules can lead to the loss of fine-grained visual details, while sequential inference introduces substantial latency. To address these limitations, we propose VLALight, a lightweight end-to-end vision-language-action framework that directly maps intersection observations and signal-phase information to discrete signal actions. To handle the multi-view nature of TSC, VLALight combines multiple directional camera views into a unified visual input and uses textual instructions to establish their correspondence with traffic movements and signal phases. This design enables direct action prediction with a compact 0.5 B-parameter model, without intermediate image-to-text descriptions or handcrafted traffic-state representations. Experiments show that VLALight delivers the best emergency-vehicle service of all compared methods, reducing pooled emergency waiting time by 21.1% over the cascaded VLMLight while running in real time on local hardware and generalizing to unseen intersection topologies and traffic-flow patterns.

</details>


### [7] [TrafficImag: A Benchmark for Counterfactual Roadside Traffic Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30722)
*Xiangyu Li,Tianyi Wang,Zhihao Dou,Christian Claudel,Zhaomiao Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TrafficImag，首个用于反事实路侧交通视频生成的基准，包含大规模路侧数据集（9022张标注图像、7043条去重视频片段和31145个以演员为中心的历史-未来样本）及可执行协议，支持行为推理、干预感知图像编辑和条件视频生成，从初始状态正确性、路线与行为有效性、交互一致性和非目标保留四个维度评估，端到端成功需四者同时满足；实验表明最强推理器宏F1达80.4%，完整条件接口将最佳生成器端到端成功率从23.3%提升至55.0%，并诊断出条件视频执行仍是主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有路侧交通数据集虽支持感知、预测和视觉问答，但未评估反事实视频生成——即修改选定演员后，生成的未来须与道路拓扑和无关交通保持一致，因此需要专门基准来填补这一空白。

Method: 构建TrafficImag基准：整合大规模路侧数据集和可执行协议；将每次干预表示为演员级程序（描述目标演员、预期行为、合法路线、交互顺序和时间约束），为异构基础模型提供统一评估接口；从初始状态正确性、路线与行为有效性、交互一致性、非目标保留四个互补有效性维度进行评估，端到端反事实仅在四维全部满足时记为成功。

Result: 在先进基础模型上，最强推理器达80.4%宏F1；完整条件接口将最佳生成器端到端成功率从23.3%提升到55.0%；Oracle研究显示条件视频执行是主要剩余瓶颈。

Conclusion: TrafficImag提供了可复现的基准，用于超越感知视频质量来评估和诊断反事实交通视频生成。

Abstract: Existing roadside traffic datasets support perception, forecasting, and visual question answering, but they do not evaluate counterfactual video generation, in which a selected actor is modified and the generated future should remain consistent with road topology and unrelated traffic. We introduce TrafficImag, the first benchmark for counterfactual roadside traffic video generation. TrafficImag combines a large-scale roadside dataset (9,022 annotated images, 7,043 deduplicated video clips, and 31,145 actor-centered history-future samples) with an executable protocol that supports behavior reasoning, intervention-aware image editing, and conditional video generation. Each intervention is represented as an actor-level program describing the target actor, intended behavior, legal route, interaction order, and temporal constraints, enabling a unified evaluation interface across heterogeneous foundation models. TrafficImag evaluates four complementary validity dimensions: initial-state correctness, route and behavior validity, interaction consistency, and non-target preservation, and considers an end-to-end counterfactual successful only when all four are satisfied. Across state-of-the-art foundation models, the strongest reasoner reaches 80.4% macro F1, the complete condition interface raises end-to-end success from 23.3% to 55.0% for the best generator. Oracle studies further show that conditional video execution is the primary remaining bottleneck. TrafficImag provides a reproducible benchmark for evaluating and diagnosing counterfactual traffic video generation beyond perceptual video quality.

</details>


### [8] [Skip the Talk, Re-Focus on Vision: Latent Reasoning for Reasoning Segmentation in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30783)
*Tianhang Guo,Yulin He,Wei Chen,Wenjuan Zhou,Yuhang Li,Xinbiao Gan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 LIRSeg，用一组紧凑的可学习潜在令牌完全替代显式思维链（CoT），用于推理分割任务。通过两阶段训练（空间对齐+GRPO分割奖励优化）以及三种信息视角机制（极端优势采样、解耦探索-稳定更新、潜在多样性增强），在保证高分割精度的同时大幅降低推理令牌数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理分割方法通常先由多模态大语言模型生成显式思维链（CoT）再进行目标定位。尽管直觉上合理，但这种显式言语推理会引发显著的注意力干扰：冗余文本令牌在感知令牌生成阶段干扰注意力，同时增加了视觉令牌间的有效距离，从而损害分割精度与效率。

Method: 提出 LIRSeg，以紧凑的可学习潜在令牌完全替代显式 CoT。训练分为两个阶段：(1) 空间对齐阶段，将潜在令牌锚定到与目标相关的视觉证据上；(2) GRPO 阶段，以分割奖励进一步优化潜在令牌。为增强紧凑令牌的信息量，引入三种互补机制：极端优势采样（选择信息增益高的训练信号）、解耦探索-稳定更新（学习互补表示）、潜在多样性增强（防止表示坍塌）。

Result: 在多个基准上，LIRSeg 同时提升分割精度与推理效率。相比 VisionReasoner 基线，ReasonSeg 上 gIoU 绝对提升 4.9%，MUSE 上提升 7.1%，MMR 上提升 4.7%，同时推理令牌数量减少约 16 倍。

Conclusion: 显式 CoT 的言语推理并非推理分割的必要条件；紧凑的可学习潜在令牌能够在消除注意力干扰的同时兼顾精度与效率，为推理分割提供了一种更优的范式。

Abstract: Reasoning segmentation aims to interpret implicit textual queries and enable fine-grained visual perception, which is critical for applications such as human-computer interaction and embodied agents. Existing methods typically generate explicit Chain-of-Thought (CoT) by multimodal large language models (MLLMs) before localizing the target. Although intuitive, such explicit verbal reasoning introduces substantial attention interference: redundant textual tokens disrupt attention during perception-token generation and also increase the effective distance between visual tokens. To address this issue, we propose LIRSeg, which fully replaces explicit CoT with a compact set of learnable latent tokens for reasoning segmentation. LIRSeg is trained in two stages: spatial alignment grounds the latent tokens in object-relevant visual evidence, and GRPO further optimizes them with segmentation rewards. To make these compact latent tokens more informative, we introduce three complementary mechanisms from an information perspective: extreme-advantage sampling for selecting informative training signals, decoupled exploration-stability updates for learning complementary representations, and latent diversity amplification for preventing representational collapse. Extensive experiments on benchmarks demonstrate that LIRSeg consistently improves both segmentation accuracy and reasoning efficiency. Compared with the VisionReasoner baseline, LIRSeg achieves absolute gIoU improvements of 4.9% on ReasonSeg, 7.1% on MUSE, and 4.7% on MMR, while achieving a approximately 16x reduction in reasoning tokens. Code is available in supplementary materials.

</details>


### [9] [FuseReg: Regularizing Layer Fusion Mitigates the Reconstruction-Generation Gap in Representation Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31620)
*Hongyang Du,Yunfei Xie,Junjie Ye,Jiawei Yang,Xiaoyan Cong,Haodong Zhang,Yongchao Huang,Haiyu Wu,Zongxia Li,Shihang Gui,Dawei Liu,Runhao Li,Jingcheng Ni,Chen Wei,Randall Balestriero,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Summary generation failed

Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Abstract: Representation autoencoders (RAEs) reuse features from a pretrained visual encoder as reconstruction and diffusion latents, integrating strong visual representations into image generation. However, RAEs still need to decide which encoder layers form the shared latent space for the generator and pixel decoder. This choice involves a trade-off. Shallower layers tend to preserve fine pixel details better, while deeper layers tend to yield better generation metrics. A fixed heuristic layer fusion therefore couples two stages that benefit from different information. We introduce FuseReg, which replaces heuristic feature selection with training over random subsets of encoder layers. We theoretically analyze the underlying mechanism: subset sampling explicitly penalizes sensitivity to cross-layer disagreement. On ImageNet-256 with DINOv3-L, a single FuseReg decoder reconstructs from full, sparse, and single-layer fusions without retraining, achieving higher PSNR than decoders specialized to fixed fusions. This flexibility also benefits generation: decoder replacement alone reduces unguided gFID by 27% with an unchanged RAEv2 DiT-XL generator. The same regularization principle extends to diffusion training, with joint regularization of both stages reducing unguided gFID by 29% on DiT-Base. These results show that training downstream models for layer-fusion robustness narrows the reconstruction-generation gap without modifying the pretrained encoder.

</details>

### [10] [A Mechanistic Study of AI-Text Detection Neurons in Frozen BERT: Sparse Probing and Activation Patching on RAID](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30287)
*Paweł Blicharz,Miłosz Grunwald*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究冻结BERT-base-uncased编码器中支持AI文本检测的神经元。采用L1-to-L2稀疏探测协议，对RAID基准中六个生成器的CLS隐藏状态维度（9,216个神经元）进行分析，发现每个生成器仅需不到1%的稳定神经元即可保留大部分检测精度。双向激活修补证实这些神经元的因果相关性，均值消融显示信号冗余分布。跨生成器分析揭示指令微调生成器在最后层集中更多稳定神经元，且训练集外泛化表明可固定一个小子空间用于检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AI文本检测器在标准基准上取得高精度，但其内部表征机制尚不明确。理解哪些神经元驱动检测有助于解释检测器的行为、提升可解释性，并可能指导更高效、可泛化的检测器设计。

Method: 在冻结的BERT-base-uncased编码器上，使用RAID基准（涵盖六个生成器：纯基座模型和指令微调模型）进行实验。对所有9,216个CLS隐藏状态维度（12层×768）应用Gurnee等人（2023）的L1-to-L2稀疏探测协议，提取每个生成器的稳定神经元集（<1%）。通过折叠和种子验证稳定性，用双向激活修补评估因果相关性，用均值消融评估冗余性，并进行跨生成器分析和留一族评估。

Result: 每个生成器均能恢复一组小于1%的稳定神经元，该集合保留大部分完整特征检测精度。双向激活修补显示这些神经元翻转预测的频率比随机集合高一个数量级；均值消融后精度几乎不变，表明信号冗余分布。跨生成器分析显示指令微调生成器在最后一层集中30-36%的稳定神经元，而两个基座生成器均低于14%。留一族评估显示所选神经元在未见生成器族上保留86-94%的完整特征上限精度。

Conclusion: AI文本检测的关键信号集中在少量且跨生成器稳定的神经元子空间中，且指令微调引入的模型在最后一层有更强的表征足迹。检测器可在固定的小子空间上运行，无需针对每个生成器重新识别神经元，同时保持较高的泛化能力。该工作为检测器的可解释性和高效部署提供了新视角。

Abstract: AI-generated text detectors achieve high accuracy on standard benchmarks, yet the internal representations that drive these predictions remain poorly understood. We study which neurons in a frozen BERT-base-uncased encoder support AI-text detection, using the RAID benchmark across six generators spanning pure-base and instruction-tuned models. We apply the L1-to-L2 sparse-probing protocol of Gurnee et al. (2023) to all 9,216 CLS hidden-state dimensions (12 layers x 768), which we call neurons. The procedure recovers a stable set of under 1% of neurons per generator, consistent across folds and seeds; a probe restricted to that set retains most of the full-feature detection accuracy. Bidirectional activation patching confirms this set's causal relevance: in both directions it flips predictions an order of magnitude more often than size-matched random sets. Mean-ablating the same neurons leaves accuracy largely intact; the signal is therefore redundantly distributed. Cross-generator analysis reveals a bipartite structure: instruction-tuned generators concentrate 30-36% of stable neurons in BERT's final layer while both base generators fall below 14%, consistent with a layer-12 footprint of post-training alignment. Leave-one-family-out evaluation shows the selected neurons retain 86-94% of the full-feature ceiling on unseen generator families, so a detector can operate on a small fixed subspace without re-identifying neurons per generator.

</details>


### [11] [Not All Memories Are Equal: Hierarchical Collaborative Memory for Validity-Aware Retrieval in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30289)
*Yufei Shi,Rujing Yao,Ang Li,Yang Wu,Zhuoren Jiang,Xiaozhong Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出HiCoMER框架，用于LLM智能体中的层次化协作记忆管理与有效性感知检索，通过维护记忆中团队与个体记忆的有效性来避免检索到过时或冲突的记忆，从而提升协作场景下的问答质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆增强系统将所有存储记忆视作平面池进行检索，按语义相关性、重要性或时效性排序，忽略了记忆的层次结构（团队记忆与个体记忆）和有效性的动态演变。这导致检索结果中常出现语义相关但已过时或与当前团队共识相冲突的个体记忆，无法为LLM智能体提供当前有效的记忆支撑。

Method: HiCoMER包含三个组件：1) 层次化记忆冲突更新器（Hierarchical Memory Conflict Updater），维护团队与个体记忆的有效性；2) 有效性感知记忆检索器（Validity-Aware Memory Retriever），仅检索仍然有效的记忆；3) 记忆基础答案生成器（Memory-Grounded Answer Generator），基于有效记忆生成问答回复。此外构建了两个新的协作场景记忆基础问答数据集用于评估。

Result: 在两个新构建的协作问答数据集上，HiCoMER一致性地优于强基线方法，具体体现在减少过时检索、保留当前团队共识以及提升下游问答质量三个方面。

Conclusion: 团队协作场景下，记忆系统应显式建模团队记忆与个体记忆的层次结构及动态有效性，优先检索当前有效的记忆，而非简单地从全部记忆中按相关性或重要度检索，这能显著改善LLM智能体的协作问答表现。

Abstract: In team collaboration scenarios, memory is heterogeneous and continually evolving. Team memories capture collective decisions, protocols, and current consensus, while individual memories preserve member-specific observations, execution traces, and intermediate progress. Existing memory-augmented systems typically retrieve from all stored memories as a flat pool, ranking them by semantic relevance, importance, or recency without modeling hierarchical structure or evolving validity. As a result, they often surface semantically relevant but outdated or conflicting memories, especially individual memories that no longer align with current team consensus, instead of prioritizing currently valid memories. This is particularly problematic when collaborative LLM agents answer user questions, since their responses should be grounded in valid memories. We propose HiCoMER, a framework for hierarchical collaborative memory management and validity-aware retrieval in LLM agents. HiCoMER first maintains the validity of team and individual memories and then retrieves memories that remain valid, rather than retrieving directly from all stored memories. It consists of three components: a Hierarchical Memory Conflict Updater, a Validity-Aware Memory Retriever, and a Memory-Grounded Answer Generator. To evaluate HiCoMER, we construct two new datasets for memory-grounded question answering in collaborative settings. Experiments on both datasets show that HiCoMER consistently outperforms strong baselines by reducing outdated retrieval, preserving current team consensus, and improving downstream QA quality.

</details>


### [12] [Auditing and Repairing LLM-as-Judge Failures in a Production Text-to-SQL Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30290)
*Haowei Liu,Hsin-Tai Wu,Yi Fang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性审计了生产环境text-to-SQL流水线中LLM-as-judge（大模型裁判）与人类标注者的一致性。结果显示，部署的gpt-4o-mini裁判在分歧强化集上Cohen's kappa仅为0.04，在均匀随机抽查上为0.42，且过度标记了77.1%的人为忠实样例。研究发现一种名为GRADE-HALLUCINATION的单一机制是过度标记的主要来源。自托管的Qwen3.6-27B替代方案（kappa=0.72）与Claude Opus 4.7（kappa=0.71）性能相当，但成本仅为其约1/300。集成策略并非免费获益：弱裁判搭配强裁判反而降低一致性，而三个强裁判在一致路由下达到kappa=0.79及89.7%自动覆盖率。跨领域应用时，该审计方法在BIRD金融数据集上标记了25.5%的专家标注金标准SQL为潜在问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境的text-to-SQL流水线普遍以LLM-as-judge作为最终评判环节，但该裁判与人类标注者之间的一致性从未被实际测量过。这种未经审计的自动评估机制可能在部署中产生系统性偏差，因此作者对自家部署的裁判进行了首次一致性核查，以揭示其真实可靠性。

Method: 作者设计了一套审计方案（audit recipe）：构建分歧强化集（disagreement-enriched set）与均匀随机抽查集，用Cohen's kappa量化部署裁判（gpt-4o-mini）与两位作者人工金标准的一致性。进一步分析过度标记（over-flagging）的机制，将其归因于GRADE-HALLUCINATION。随后评估替代方案：自托管Qwen3.6-27B、Claude Opus 4.7，以及多种集成策略（弱强搭配、三强裁判+一致路由）。最后将该审计方法跨领域迁移到BIRD金融数据集上进行验证。研究附带预注册和开源代码。

Result: 部署的gpt-4o-mini裁判在与双作者金标准对比时，分歧强化集上kappa仅0.04，均匀随机抽查集为0.42，且在强化集中过度标记了77.1%的人为忠实（FAITHFUL）样例。自托管Qwen3.6-27B取得kappa=0.72，与Claude Opus 4.7（kappa=0.71）处于相同水平；尽管n=96的正面比较统计功效不足（underpowered），但对于部署决策而言，Qwen每调用成本约为Claude的1/300，实际影响显著。集成方面，弱裁判加强裁判的组合使一致性退化，而三强裁判在一致路由（unanimity routing）下达到kappa=0.79及89.7%自动覆盖率。跨领域应用时，审计方案在BIRD金融数据集的专家金标准SQL上标记了25.5%为疑似问题。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了"未经审计的LLM裁判不可信"这一关键事实：部署中的gpt-4o-mini一致性极差（kappa=0.04），且其偏差高度集中在GRADE-HALLUCINATION这一单一机制上，提示可通过针对性修复改善。自托管开源模型（Qwen）能以极低成本达到与顶级商业模型相当的评判质量，是更具性价比的部署选择。集成并非免费增益，需采用"多强裁判+一致路由"策略才能提升一致性；成本敏感的部署决策可在统计功效不足的情况下仍做出合理选择。该审计方案具备跨领域可迁移性，可为其他text-to-SQL生产系统提供标准化的裁判可信度审计范式。

Abstract: Production text-to-SQL pipelines often end with an LLM-as-judge whose agreement with human annotators has never actually been measured. When we checked ours, the deployed gpt-4o-mini judge agreed with two-author gold at only Cohen's kappa = 0.04 on a disagreement-enriched set and 0.42 on a uniform-random spot-check, over-flagging 77.1% of the human-FAITHFUL cases in the enriched set. Most of its over-flags trace back to a single mechanism we call GRADE-HALLUCINATION. A self-hosted Qwen3.6-27B replacement (kappa = 0.72) lands in the same range as Claude Opus 4.7 (kappa = 0.71); the head-to-head is underpowered at n = 96, but for the deployment decision that hardly matters, since Qwen costs roughly 1/300 as much per call. Ensembling does not help for free. Pairing the weak judge with a stronger one degrades agreement, whereas three strong judges under unanimity routing reach kappa = 0.79 at 89.7% auto-coverage. Applied out-of-domain, the same audit recipe flags 25.5% of BIRD-financial's expert-authored gold SQLs as candidate gold-SQL issues under our annotation protocol. Code and pre-registration are at https://github.com/JamesL404/synca-audit.

</details>


### [13] [A Survey on Fake Review Detection: From Pre-trained Language Models to Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30292)
*Fanji Yang,Huiyao Chen,Xi Yu,Meishan Zhang,Xiaohong Xiao,Mingsen Deng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文是一篇从信息融合视角对虚假评论检测领域进行系统综述的文章，覆盖2018年至2026年初的211项研究。按证据来源和融合层级组织现有工作（评论文本、情感、评分行为、时间元数据、用户-商品图、多模态内容、外部知识及LLM生成信号），梳理了从传统机器学习、深度学习到基于PLM和LLM方法的演进，并分析了Amazon、Yelp、OpSpam基准族上的性能趋势及其局限，最后指出对抗生成、跨域迁移、不确定性融合、缺失源鲁棒性、可解释性和可信评测等开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线评论深刻影响消费者决策、平台治理与企业声誉，而虚假评论通过注入欺骗性证据破坏评分系统、推荐管道和公众信任机制。大语言模型的兴起使该问题呈双向变化：LLM能生成流畅且上下文感知的欺骗性评论，同时PLM和LLM也为检测提供更强的语义表示。因此需要系统梳理该领域从信息融合角度的研究进展，揭示方法演进与评测现状。

Method: 作者采用信息融合视角组织综述：按证据来源（评论文本、情感、评分行为、时间元数据、用户-商品图、多模态内容、外部知识、LLM生成信号）和融合层级对211项研究（2018至2026年初）进行分类，并梳理从传统机器学习、深度学习到PLM/LLM方法的发展脉络，考察不同方法如何组合文本、行为、结构及多模态证据。

Result: 综述分析了Amazon、Yelp和OpSpam主流基准族上报告的性能趋势，指出由于标签构建流程、数据划分和评估协议不同导致的局限。同时覆盖了从传统方法到PLM/LLM方法的演进全貌及各证据融合方式。

Conclusion: 作者总结出若干开放问题：对抗生成攻击、跨域迁移、不确定性感知融合、缺失证据源鲁棒性、可解释性，以及对AI生成欺骗性内容的可信评测。这些构成未来研究的主要方向。

Abstract: Online reviews shape consumer decisions, platform governance, and corporate reputation.Fake reviews compromise this information channel by injecting deceptive evidence into rating systems, recommendation pipelines, and public trust mechanisms.The rise of large language models, or LLMs, has changed the problem in two directions.LLMs can generate fluent and context-aware deceptive reviews, while pre-trained language models, or PLMs, and LLMs also provide stronger semantic representations for detection.This survey reviews fake review detection from an information fusion perspective, covering 211 studies published from 2018 to early 2026.We organize existing work by evidence source and fusion level, covering review text, sentiment, rating behavior, temporal metadata, user-product graphs, multimodal content, external knowledge, and LLM-generated signals.We trace the development from traditional machine learning and deep learning to PLM-based and LLM-based methods, and examine how different approaches combine textual, behavioral, structural, and multimodal evidence.We also analyze reported performance trends on widely used Amazon, Yelp, and OpSpam benchmark families, while noting the limitations caused by different label construction procedures, data splits, and evaluation protocols.Finally, we identify open problems in adversarial generation, cross-domain transfer, uncertainty-aware fusion, missing-source robustness, interpretability, and trustworthy evaluation for AI-generated deceptive content.

</details>


### [14] [Cartograph: Federated Tool Discovery with Operator-Attested Retrieval for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30293)
*Justice Owusu Agyemang,Michael Agyare,Kwame Opuni-Boachie Obour Agyekum,Kwame Agyeman-Prempeh Agyekum,Francisca Adoma Acheampong,Jerry John Kponyo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Cartograph 是一个联邦 MCP 代理，通过将 agent 可见的工具发现从 O(n) 目录遍历降为 O(k) 渐进式披露，解决了 MCP 中工具定义加载开销过大的问题。它结合了运营商认证的能力卡片（Ed25519 签名）、三层混淆簇分析（Rift）以及两阶段检索（先对服务器排序再对工具排序）。在 22 服务器、374 工具的部署中，仅暴露 3 个代理工具而非 374 个定义；49 查询基准上 R@5 达 0.816（高出 Jaccard 基线 0.592），top-5 发现交换仅用 475 tokens（对比完整目录的 42,450），延迟开销仅 5ms（0.8%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCP 使 AI 智能体能够发现并调用工具，但随着连接的目录增多，加载每个工具定义的开销变得昂贵。其核心动机是解决工具发现的可扩展性问题——将昂贵的目录遍历转变为渐进式、分层级的工具披露，同时保证描述的可信度（运营商认证）和检索精度。

Method: Cartograph 采用三种机制：(1) 运营商认证的能力卡片——在部署运营商控制下生成的 Ed25519 签名描述，替代发布者的排名文案；(2) Rift——三层混淆簇分析，包括密度聚类、查询边界分析和 token 诊断；(3) 两阶段检索——先对服务器排序，再对工具排序，从而暴露 3 个代理工具而非全部定义。

Result: 在 22 服务器、374 工具部署中，Cartograph 仅暴露 3 个代理工具而非 374 个定义。49 查询作者构建基准上 R@5 为 0.816，优于 Jaccard 关键字基线的 0.592；top-5 发现交换仅用 475 tokens（对比完整目录 42,450）。Rift 识别出 49 个混淆簇，其中 4 个在 bootstrap 生成卡片中为 HIGH 风险。119 个 LLM 生成的描述比较消除了观察到的零距离簇，但混合卡片生成机制会降低 R@5。十次网关测试相对直接 stdio MCP 调用平均增加 5ms 延迟（0.8%）。

Conclusion: Cartograph 与代码执行方法互补：它控制哪些工具描述被呈现，并为每次查询记录用于排序的描述来源（来源追踪）。它有效降低了 MCP 工具发现的开销与延迟，同时通过签名能力卡片保证了描述的认证可信度，支持大规模联邦工具目录的可扩展发现。

Abstract: The Model Context Protocol (MCP) enables AI agents to discover and call tools, but loading every definition becomes expensive as connected catalogs grow. We present Cartograph, a federated MCP proxy that changes agent-visible tool discovery from $O(n)$ catalog traversal to $O(k)$ progressive disclosure. Cartograph combines three mechanisms: (1) operator-attested capability cards, Ed25519-signed descriptions generated under the deploying operator's control rather than ranked publisher copy; (2) Rift, a three-layer confusable-cluster analysis comprising density clustering, query-margin analysis, and token diagnosis; and (3) two-stage retrieval, which ranks servers before tools. On a 22-server, 374-tool deployment, Cartograph exposes three proxy tools instead of 374 definitions. A 49-query author-constructed benchmark yields R@5 of 0.816, compared with 0.592 for a Jaccard keyword baseline, while a measured top-5 discovery exchange uses 475 tokens rather than 42,450 under the stated full-catalog accounting. Rift identifies 49 confusable clusters, including four HIGH-risk clusters in bootstrap-generated cards. An exploratory comparison of 119 LLM-generated descriptions removes the observed zero-distance cluster but shows that mixing card-generation regimes can reduce R@5. Gateway measurements over ten trials add 5ms mean latency (0.8%) relative to direct stdio MCP calls. Cartograph is complementary to code-execution approaches: it controls which tool descriptions are surfaced and records the provenance of the descriptions used for ranking for each query.

</details>


### [15] [SlideLab: Audience-Centered Scientific Slide Generation and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30294)
*Vidushee Vats,Karun Sharma,Yuxia Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SlideLab是一个无需训练的multi-agent框架，用于从研究论文自动生成科学演示文稿。它通过规划叙述、内容规划、视觉生成、布局优化和真实性验证等代理协作构建幻灯片，并在盲测中被77%的论文所偏好，同时推理token消耗比最强开源基线少约4倍。论文还提出了ConfArena，一个模拟会议室并逐张评估演示文稿的观众导向评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学演示不仅仅是研究论文的摘要，需要以连贯的序列呈现工作、清晰地解释核心思想并帮助观众跟随。现有自动生成演示文稿的系统在这些方面表现不足，需要更完善的框架来生成高质量的学术演示文稿。

Method: SlideLab采用无训练的multi-agent架构，首先规划演示叙述，然后通过内容规划、视觉生成、布局优化和grounding验证等多个代理来构建并迭代优化共享的幻灯片组。同时提出ConfArena评估框架，模拟会议室场景逐张评估演示文稿，检测伪造数字、劣化图表、遗漏幻灯片和顺序错乱等问题。

Result: 在盲测人类偏好研究中，SlideLab在77%的论文上优于开源和商业系统，且使用的推理token数量约比最强开源基线少4倍。ConfArena与人类系统排名一致，并能有效检测注入的演示问题。

Conclusion: SlideLab通过多代理协作和迭代优化，能够以较高效率生成更受人类偏好的科学演示文稿；ConfArena提供了一种与人类判断一致的自动化评估方法，为生成式演示系统提供了可靠的评测基准。

Abstract: Scientific presentations are more than summaries of research papers. They need to present the work in a coherent sequence, explain the main ideas clearly, and help the audience follow the presentation. We present SlideLab, a training-free multi-agent framework for generating scientific presentations from research papers. SlideLab first plans the presentation narrative, then builds and iteratively refines a shared slide deck using agents for content planning, visual generation, layout refinement, and grounding verification. In a blind human preference study, SlideLab was preferred over both open-source and commercial systems on 77% of papers while using roughly 4 times fewer inference tokens than the strongest open-source baseline. We also introduce ConfArena, an audience-oriented evaluation framework that simulates a conference room and assesses presentations slide by slide. ConfArena matches human system rankings and detects injected presentation problems, including falsified numbers, degraded figures, dropped slides, and shuffled slide order.

</details>


### [16] [SignTrace: Describe a Sign, Find the Word](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30295)
*Zengji Tu,Xingye Zhu,Ningjing Wang,Tingyi Huang,Yangjunfeng Zhu,Dai Wan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SignTrace是一个面向中国手语词典的反向查找系统，通过自然语言描述（如"掌心向上挥动"）帮助学习者识别不熟悉的手势。系统集成了LLM驱动的词典增强、动作提取、词典风格改写、七通道检索和候选重排序，在6699个词条上实现了94.0%的Hit@1和97.4%的Hit@9。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手语学习者常能记住某个手势的动作，却不知道其含义或正式特征编码，传统词典难以从自然语言动作描述反向查询到对应词条。这是一个长期存在的"反向查找"难题，缺乏有效的自然语言访问入口。

Method: SignTrace整合了五个关键组件：(1) 基于LLM的词典增强，丰富词典词条描述；(2) 动作提取，从描述中抽取动作特征；(3) 词典风格改写，使查询与词典表述对齐；(4) 七通道检索，从多维度匹配候选；(5) 候选重排序，优化最终结果排序。系统覆盖6,699个词条并已部署上线。

Result: 在500条动作描述查询的基准测试上，Hit@1达到94.0%，Hit@9为97.4%，平均倒数排名为0.9540。重排序将Hit@1从71.8%提升至94.0%。中位查询处理时间为13.37秒（支持6个并发查询）。用户试用获得积极非正式反馈。

Conclusion: SignTrace通过将日常动作描述连接至已收录的手语词条和含义，为手语学习者识别不熟悉手势提供了实用工具。但基准测试中的查询表述源自词典措辞且存在先验选择，限制了对用户独立生成描述的泛化能力。

Abstract: Identifying an unfamiliar sign is difficult when a learner remembers its movement but does not know its meaning or formal feature codes. SignTrace addresses this longstanding reverse-lookup problem through natural-language access to a Chinese sign-language dictionary. The system integrates LLM-based dictionary enrichment, action extraction, dictionary-style rewriting, seven-channel retrieval, and candidate reranking over 6,699 entries. It has been deployed for user trials and has received positive informal feedback. Evaluation on a dictionary-derived benchmark of 500 movement-description queries yields 94.0% Hit@1, 97.4% Hit@9, and a mean reciprocal rank of 0.9540. Reranking increases Hit@1 from 71.8% to 94.0%, while component analyses show the contribution of enriched entry descriptions. Median query-processing time is 13.37 seconds with six concurrent queries. By connecting everyday movement descriptions to documented signs and meanings, SignTrace provides a practical tool for identifying unfamiliar signs. Dictionary-derived wording and prior selection within the benchmark limit generalization to descriptions independently produced by users.

</details>


### [17] [Bootstrapping Conversational Recommendation Agents At Spotify: Synthetic Data Generation and Self-Improvement Loops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30297)
*Enrico Palumbo,Alexandre Tamborrino,Victor Ode,Ben Lacker,Adrià Casas Escoda,Jeremy Hopple,Marcus Better,James Leoni,Hugo Galvão,Hugues Bouchard,Mounia Lalmas,José Luis Redondo García,Abenezer Abebe,Ann Clifton,Anton Blomberg,Henrik Lindström,Dani Doro,Christine Doig Cardet*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于构建对话式推荐代理（conversational recommendation agents）的多轮合成数据生成管道和自我改进循环。该方法通过将单轮提示转换为逼真的多轮对话来实现上线前的系统评估，并结合基于方差的对比优化与编码代理驱动的迭代改进来修复规划和工具调用错误。该系统已在Spotify生产环境中部署，在高度优化的手动提示基础上质量提升8%，在线A/B测试显示用户收听时长+14%、周活跃用户+5%、跳过率降低5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话式推荐代理的核心挑战在于代理规划（如何选择、排序和调用工具），尤其在冷启动场景下缺乏真实用户交互数据。现有方法难以在系统上线前进行系统化评估，且代理规划和工具调用的错误难以自动识别和修复，迫切需要一种无需真实数据即可加速开发迭代的实用框架。

Method: 提出两条核心组件：(1) 多轮合成数据生成管道：将单轮提示转换为逼真的多轮对话，支持上线前的系统化评估；(2) 自我改进循环：结合基于方差的对比优化（variance-based contrastive optimization）与编码代理驱动的迭代精炼，自动识别并修复规划和工具使用错误。整个系统在生产环境中部署并持续迭代。

Result: 在高度优化的手动提示基础上，该方法进一步提升了8%的质量。在线A/B测试显示：用户收听时长增加14%，周活跃用户提升5%，跳过率降低5%，显著优于仅支持会话精炼的先前系统。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个在工业界加速对话式推荐代理开发的实用框架，验证了合成数据生成与自我改进循环相结合的方法在冷启动场景下构建高效代理规划的有效性，且已在Spotify成功生产化。

Abstract: Conversational recommendation agents are a new paradigm for content discovery, enabling users to express complex intents through natural language (e.g., "recommend Italian indie artists I haven't heard before"). A central challenge in building such agents is optimizing agent planning -- deciding how to select, sequence, and invoke tools -- particularly in cold-start settings where real user interactions are not yet available. We introduce a pipeline for multi-turn synthetic data generation and a self-improvement loop to address this challenge. The synthetic data pipeline transforms single-turn prompts into realistic multi-turn conversations, enabling systematic evaluation before launch. The self-improvement loop combines variance-based contrastive optimization with iterative refinement through a coding agent, automatically identifying and fixing planning and tool-use errors. Our approach improves quality by +8% on top of a highly optimized manual prompt. The system has been productionized and significantly accelerated iteration cycles for the launch of a conversational recommendation agent at Spotify. Online A/B tests demonstrate its effectiveness, with +14% user listening, +5% increase in weekly active users, and a 5% reduction in skip rate compared to a prior experience supporting only session refinement. This work provides a practical framework for accelerating the development of conversational recommendation agents in industry.

</details>


### [18] [A Unified Account of Concepts and Chunks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30414)
*Karthik Singaravadivelan,Pat Langley*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文回顾了Cobweb这一分类与概念形成的计算模型，并提出了一个融合"块"（chunks）及其习得的扩展理论。该理论对模态无承诺，适用于任何可分解为元素及元素间关系的经验。作者实现了该理论的系统Trellis，并以上下文无关语法（CFG）作为测试平台，在三个合成语法上的实验表明系统能够表示句法知识、用于解析和生成句子，并从样本解析中学习组合结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 认知心理学中关于概念（categories）的编码、使用与学习研究，以及关于块（chunks）的表征、识别与习得研究几乎彼此脱节，这给统一认知理论带来了挑战。本文旨在弥合这一鸿沟，提出一个同时涵盖概念与块的理论框架。

Method: 本文基于Cobweb模型提出扩展理论，将块及其习得纳入其中。理论对模态不作承诺，适用于任何可分解为元素和关系的经验。作者实现了Trellis系统，并以上下文无关语法为测试平台，因CFG同时涉及概念类元素和块类元素；随后在三个合成语法上进行实验验证。

Result: 实验结果表明，Trellis系统能够表征句法知识，利用这些知识进行句子的解析与生成，并能从样本解析中学习组合结构。系统在三个合成语法上均展示了上述能力。

Conclusion: 本文通过整合概念与块的研究，提出了一个统一的认知计算理论，并借助Trellis在语法学习任务中验证了其可行性。作者还讨论了与概念和块相关的既有工作，并指出了未来研究方向。

Abstract: Cognitive psychology has studied how people encode, use, and learn concepts that describe categories, and how they represent, recognize, and acquire chunks for familiar patterns of elements. The literatures on these two topics are nearly disjoint, which poses a challenge for unified theories of cognition. In this paper, we review Cobweb, a computational account of categorization and concept formation, and propose an extended theory that incorporates chunks and their acquisition. The theory makes no commitments about modality, applying to any experience that decomposes into elements and relations among them. We also present \trellis/, an implementation of this theory, and illustrate its application to learning context-free grammars, which we adopt as a testbed because they involve both concept-like and chunk-like elements. In addition, we report experimental results on three synthetic grammars that demonstrate the system's ability to represent syntactic knowledge, use it to parse and generate sentences, and learn compositional structures from sample parses. We conclude by discussing related work on concepts and chunks, along with directions for future research in the area.

</details>


### [19] [CARGO: Context-Aware Retrieval-Gated Evaluation of Agentic AI in Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30471)
*Mukul Chhabra,Shail Patel,Luigi Medrano*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对部署在动态实体（如工单、资产、账户）上的智能体系统，标准参考答案式LLM评判存在“参考实例分歧”（RID）问题：参考答案虽应用了正确流程但针对不同实体，字面评判会误把正确回答判为幻觉。本文提出CARGO框架，将检索到的参考视为程序性范例、以实时实例观察到的上下文作为事实判断依据，并为每条主张赋予“支持/矛盾/不可验证”三态标签、仅惩罚矛盾，同时用检索置信度对评判进行门控（选择性预测）。配套的CARGO-Bench扰动式诊断套件表明，传统参考式评判会错误惩罚100%的正确“实体移植”答案且判别力近零（DI≈0），而CARGO消除这些误罚（0/50）并保留近乎完整的矛盾召回率（50/50与49/50），DI升至0.58。但该框架自身也有局限：保护实体值的宽容度抑制了对程序性篡改的检测（召回20%），事后修复无法弥补差距，人工标注研究也呈现同样盲区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在真实部署的智能体系统中，评测对象是动态实体，而最接近的参考答案往往将正确流程应用于“不同”的实体。此时以参考答案为目标的字面评判会把正确的标识符、日期和状态差异当作错误或幻觉来惩罚（即RID失败模式），导致评判器在关键生产场景下既过度惩罚又缺乏判别力。

Method: 提出CARGO框架：①将检索到的参考作为程序性范例，以实时实例的观察上下文作为事实判断依据；②为每条主张赋予 supported/contradicted/unverifiable 三态，仅对矛盾加以惩罚；③以检索置信度对评测进行门控，将生产评测转化为选择性预测。同时构建CARGO-Bench扰动式诊断套件，其真值由构造保证（ground truth by construction），用以区分“宽容度”与“判别力”。并设计了rubric-swap对照实验及含书面指南和仲裁的LLM-as-annotator研究。

Result: 在CARGO-Bench上（246项、两种评判模型、7,872次评判），传统参考式评判对100%的正确实体移植答案给出误罚且判别指数DI≈0（无信息量）；直接把实时事实塞给评判器而不重构也无改变。CARGO将误罚降至0/50，同时保留近乎完全的矛盾召回（50/50和49/50），DI提升至0.58 [0.48, 0.68]；rubric-swap对照表明大部分效果来自“上下文锚定的维度定义”。CARGO同时也暴露出自身设计的局限：保护实体值的宽容度使程序性篡改的检测率仅20%，事后修复无法弥合差距，LLM-as-annotator研究（书面指南+仲裁）存在同样的盲区。

Conclusion: 参考实例式LLM评判在动态实体智能体场景下不可靠；CARGO通过三态主张判定+实时上下文锚定+置信度门控，在消除RID误罚的同时维持较强的矛盾检测能力与判别力，但宽容度设计换来了对程序性篡改的感知盲区，说明评估权衡（leniency vs. discrimination）需要显式刻画。作者发布了预注册协议，拟将评测扩展到专家一致性、风险覆盖率与生产流量成本等维度。

Abstract: Reference-based LLM-as-a-judge evaluation assumes the reference answer is the target. In deployed agentic systems that operate over dynamic entities (support cases, assets, accounts), the closest available reference typically applies the correct procedure to a different entity, so a literal judge penalizes different identifiers, dates, and statuses as errors or hallucinations. We name this failure mode reference-instance divergence (RID). We propose CARGO, a framework that (i) treats retrieved references as procedural exemplars and grounds factual judgments in the live instance's observed context, (ii) assigns each claim a three-way status (supported, contradicted, unverifiable) and penalizes only contradictions, and (iii) gates evaluation by retrieval confidence, casting production evaluation as selective prediction. We introduce CARGO-Bench, a perturbation-based diagnostic suite with ground truth by construction that separates leniency from discrimination. On CARGO-Bench (246 items, two judge models, 7,872 judgments), the standard reference-based judge penalizes 100% of correct entity-transplanted answers and is uninformative (discrimination index DI ~ 0); supplying the live facts without reframing changes nothing. CARGO eliminates these false penalties (0/50) while retaining near-complete contradiction recall (50/50 and 49/50), raising DI to 0.58 [0.48, 0.68]; a rubric-swap control attributes most of the effect to context-grounded dimension definitions. CARGO also exposes a limitation of its own design: the leniency that protects entity values suppresses detection of procedural corruptions (20% recall). A post-hoc fix does not close the gap, and an LLM-as-annotator study with written guidelines and adjudication shows the same blind spot. We release a preregistered protocol for extending the evaluation to expert agreement, risk-coverage, and cost on production traffic.

</details>


### [20] [Breaking Homogeneity: Diversifying Persona Sets for Creative LLM Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30492)
*Sang Bin Moon,Nicole Cho,Daniel Borrajo,Sumitra Ganesh,Abolfazl Hashemi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究通过"人格多样化"（persona diversification）机制来降低大语言模型（LLM）在开放式任务中的同质化输出，将人格多样化形式化为集合层面的条件建模问题，区分了"选择vs生成人格"与"空间填充vs前沿搜索多样性"两个正交设计维度，并提出四种方法（覆盖与分散子集选择、均匀覆盖采样、进化人格生成）。在AUT、Infinity-Chat和DAT上验证了方法有效性，其中进化人格生成在AUT上将响应多样性提升78.8%、原创性26.1%、灵活性49.5%、整体创造性13.9%，同时保持98.5%的有效性，并能与创意优化提示组合进一步增强效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在开放式任务中常产生同质化响应，这种同质性会导致"群体思维"——思想向单一且可能次优的决策收敛。作者旨在通过人格多样化来激发模型的发散性输出，从而缓解这一群体思维问题。

Method: 作者将人格多样化形式化为集合级条件建模问题，研究两个正交设计维度：(1) 选择人格（覆盖/分散子集选择）vs 生成人格（进化人格生成）；(2) 空间填充多样性 vs 前沿搜索多样性。具体实例化为四种方法：覆盖子集选择、分散子集选择、均匀覆盖采样、进化人格生成。在Alternative Uses Task (AUT)、Infinity-Chat和Divergent Association Task (DAT)上进行评估。

Result: 在AUT上，进化人格生成相较仅任务提示将响应多样性提升78.8%、原创性26.1%、灵活性49.5%、整体创造性13.9%，同时保持98.5%的有效性；在Infinity-Chat上，进化人格生成使基于人格的响应分离度相对随机人格几乎翻倍。此外，进化人格能与创意优化提示组合，进一步将响应多样性提升18.6%、创造性提升6.3%。

Conclusion: 人格集合的几何属性可作为任务无关的机制来激发LLM的发散输出，人格多样化可作为提示优化的可复用补充手段，为缓解LLM同质化与群体思维提供了一种通用解决方案。

Abstract: Language models often produce homogeneous responses to open-ended tasks; such homogeneity can spawn groupthink-the convergence of ideas toward a singular and potentially suboptimal decision. We formulate persona diversification as a set-level conditioning problem and study two orthogonal design choices: selecting versus generating personas, and space-filling versus frontier-seeking diversity. We instantiate this design space with four methods spanning coverage and dispersion subset selections, uniform-coverage sampling, and evolutionary persona generation. Evaluations on the Alternative Uses Task (AUT), Infinity-Chat, and Divergent Association Task (DAT) show the benefits of the proposed methods across tasks and creativity objectives. On AUT, evolutionary persona generation increases response diversity by 78.8%, originality by 26.1%, flexibility by 49.5%, and holistic creativity by 13.9% over task-only prompting, while maintaining 98.5% validity; on Infinity-Chat, it nearly doubles persona-induced response separation relative to random personas. Moreover, evolutionary personas compose with creativity-optimized prompting, further increasing its response diversity by 18.6% and creativity by 6.3%. These results establish persona-set geometry as a task-agnostic mechanism for eliciting divergent LLM outputs, and support persona diversification as a reusable complement to prompt optimization.

</details>


### [21] [Inquesto Score: A reliability Protocol For Voice Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30514)
*Massa Baali,Bhiksha Raj*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Inquesto Score (IS)协议，用于以可复现、可解释的方式衡量语音代理的可靠性——即在固定版本化的评估人群中，成功达成呼叫者目标且无功能性故障的呼叫百分比。该协议直接测量音频时序故障、使用情景谓词和工具痕迹评估语义及状态依赖故障，并将诊断视图与总分分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音代理越来越多地应用于交易、访问等具有后果性影响的场景，失败的交互可能带来实际损失。现有评估方法存在两个问题：缺乏可复现性（评估人群不固定）和缺乏可解释性（异构指标混合无法定位具体故障）。因此需要一种能够明确故障事件、严重程度分级并对部署管线进行全面评估的协议。

Method: IS协议定义显式故障事件和严重级别，评估部署后的完整语音管线。时序故障（抢话、延迟响应）直接从音频测量；语义和状态依赖故障通过情景谓词、工具痕迹和固定开源模型评判器评估。同时提供行为、声学鲁棒性、身份处理及说话人组别的诊断视图（不并入总分）。v0.1版本覆盖30个场景、3种声学条件、4个说话人组，每代理306通呼叫，共13种参考系统配置。

Result: 评估表明：可靠的测量需要超越文本转录的证据（音频层面的时序信息不可缺失）、明确处理部署条件（声学环境等）、以及验证用于判定结果的评估器自身的有效性。该协议、参考实现和评估记录已公开发布。

Conclusion: Inquesto Score提供了一种将可靠性总分与诊断信息分离的可复现、可解释评估框架，为语音代理在关键场景中的质量保障建立了标准化基础。

Abstract: Voice agents are increasingly deployed in workflows where failed interactions can affect transactions, access, and other consequential outcomes, creating a need for reproducible and interpretable evaluation. We introduce Inquesto Score (IS), a protocol for measuring voice-agent reliability as the percentage of calls in a fixed, versioned evaluation population that achieve the caller's goal without a functional failure or worse. Rather than combining heterogeneous metrics, IS defines explicit failure events and severity levels and evaluates the deployed voice pipeline. Timing failures, including talk-over and delayed responses, are measured directly from audio, while semantic and state-dependent failures are evaluated using scenario predicates, tool traces, and a pinned open-model judge. Diagnostic views of behavior, acoustic robustness, identity handling, and speaker groups accompany the score without being combined into it. Inquesto Score v0.1 evaluates 30 scenarios, three acoustic conditions, four speaker groups, and 306 calls per agent across 13 configurations of a reference voice-agent system. Our evaluation shows that reliable measurement requires evidence beyond transcripts, explicit treatment of deployment conditions, and validation of the evaluators used to determine outcomes. We release the protocol, reference implementation, and evaluation records.

</details>


### [22] [Probing Stability-Plasticity Tradeoffs in Agent Memory through Cognitive Experimental Paradigms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30558)
*Jiaqi Ding,Guorong Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MemProbe是一个受认知科学启发的框架，用于诊断智能体记忆系统的稳定性-可塑性权衡。它将记忆视为重构性过程，设计了四个实验范式（干扰、错误信息、巩固强度、再巩固窗口）来操纵记忆何时应被更新、保留或视为不确定，并将正确率分解为可解释的行为画像。基于56个episode的诊断套件评估了六个增量记忆系统，发现聚合分数相似的系统展现出截然不同的行为画像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体记忆评估往往将记忆行为简化为最终答案的准确率，无法揭示系统在长期记忆维护中的细粒度行为差异（如何时更新、何时保留、如何归因等），缺乏对稳定性-可塑性权衡的系统性诊断方法。

Method: 借鉴认知科学中记忆的重构性、干扰、来源可靠性、强化和再激活等核心机制，构建四个可复用的实验范式（干扰、错误信息、巩固强度、再巩固窗口），将正确率分解为更新、保留、归因和时间组织四类行为画像，并在56个episode的诊断套件上以统一协议评估六个增量记忆系统。

Result: 实验表明，聚合分数相似的系统在行为画像上存在显著差异，证明MemProbe能够将聚合性能转化为可解释的记忆维护时间曲线，有效揭示不同记忆系统的内在行为模式。

Conclusion: MemProbe提供了一个诊断透镜，将聚合性能指标转化为可解释的记忆维护行为画像，为智能体记忆系统的稳定性-可塑性权衡提供了系统化评估框架。

Abstract: Agent memory systems are increasingly used to maintain long-term user preferences, task states and evolving facts, but current evaluations often collapse memory behavior into final-answer accuracy. We introduce MemProbe, a cognitive-science-inspired framework for diagnosing stability-plasticity tradeoffs in agent memory. The framework is motivated by a core insight from cognitive memory research: memory is reconstructive and shaped by interference, source reliability, reinforcement, and reactivation. MemProbe turns this insight into four reusable experimental paradigms (interference, misinformation, consolidation strength, and reconsolidation window) that manipulate when a memory should be updated, preserved, or treated as uncertain. It further decomposes correctness into behavioral profiles that reveal how systems update, preserve, attribute, and temporally organize information. We instantiate these paradigms in a 56-episode diagnostic suite and evaluate six incremental memory systems under a unified protocol. Results show that systems with similar aggregate scores exhibit distinct behavioral profiles. MemProbe provides such a diagnostic lens, turning aggregate performance into interpretable profiles of memory maintenance over time. Code is available at https://github.com/jq-ding/MemProbe.

</details>


### [23] [The Hard Part Comes After Search: Benchmarking Web Agents on Synthesizing, Organizing, and Displaying Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30604)
*Alexander Gill,Md Farhan Ishmam,Xuyen Nguyen,Neha Bhat,Parker Henry DeYoung,Fateme Hashemi Chaleshtori,Nathan Stringham,Kenneth Marino,Ana Marasović*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了KNOWS基准，用于评估计算机使用代理在开放式、多步骤浏览器任务中的表现，这些任务要求代理检索信息、综合生成工件（文档、演示文稿、电子表格），并最终产出完整成果。实验发现前沿代理在部分成功指标上表现尚可，但在复杂长时程任务中完全成功率不足3%，且视觉步骤失败会导致最终工件不可用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算机使用代理基准未能充分评估代理作为助手的能力。一个有用的助手需要跨复杂多步骤工作流检索信息、综合生成工件、并操作程序界面产生连贯的最终产品，这要求推理与综合、复杂任务分解以及视觉和空间理解能力。现有基准缺乏对此类端到端助手能力的系统评估。

Method: 作者开发了KNOWS基准，包含开放式、复杂的浏览器任务，每个任务以产出工件告终。任务编写遵循设计规则和协议以确保满足要求。每个任务配有一个评估器程序，将确定性检查与LLM判断相结合，以平衡代理评估中丰富性、可靠性和自动化之间的权衡。

Result: 评估和分析前沿计算机使用代理与浏览器框架后发现：这些代理在部分成功指标上达到中等分数，但最佳表现者在复杂长时程任务中的完全成功率不到3%。即使代理完成了超过50%的评估步骤，视觉步骤的失败也会导致最终工件不可用。

Conclusion: 当前代理作为端到端助手的能力存在明显局限，需要在工具使用、视觉理解和长时程推理方面取得进展。该基准揭示了现有代理在真实助手场景中的不足，为该领域未来改进指明了方向。

Abstract: Existing computer-use agent benchmarks do not fully evaluate agents acting as assistants. A useful assistant retrieves information across complex, multi-step workflows, synthesizes it into artifacts (documents, presentations, spreadsheets), and navigates program interfaces to produce a coherent final product. Such workflows demand reasoning and synthesis, decomposition of complex tasks, as well as visual and spatial understanding. To study agents on workflows like these, we introduce KNOWS, a benchmark of open-ended, complex, browser-based tasks that jointly evaluate these capabilities, with each task culminating in a produced artifact. To write tasks, we develop a task design rubric and a protocol for ensuring that tasks meet the requirements. Each task is paired with an evaluator, a program that combines deterministic checks with LLM judgments to balance the richness, reliability, and automation tradeoff inherent to agent evaluation. We evaluate and analyze frontier computer-use agents and browser-based harnesses. They achieve moderate scores on partial-success metrics, but the best performer fully succeeds in fewer than 3% of our complex, long-horizon tasks. Failures on visual steps render the resulting artifacts unusable, even when agents complete more than 50% of other evaluation steps. Our results expose limitations of current agents acting as end-to-end assistants, and call for progress on tool use, visual understanding, and long-horizon reasoning.

</details>


### [24] [TRACE: Temporal Audit and Condition-aware Evaluation of Streaming Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30670)
*Yibo Ma,Qianqian Zhang,Peng Liu,Tiancheng Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 TRACE（时间审计与条件感知评估），一个条件感知的基准测试与评估框架，使流式视频理解中的证据生效时间、视觉历史维护方式和响应触发机制显式化。TRACE 结合时间审计的视觉任务、证据时机与指令相关触发标注、统一因果 Core–Adapter 协议（控制信息可用性并记录历史处理与响应事件），以及多维报告（答案质量、及时性、响应选择行为、工作量、完成度和可靠性）。在来自 517 个视频的 1240 条记录上，对 8 个公开模型/系统进行 8 种配置评估后发现：几乎相同的 QA 准确率可能掩盖完成度、答案有效性及生成工作量上的显著差异；主动式（proactive）性能可分为响应质量、响应延迟、误报（目标窗口无效但仍存在后续窗口时发出响应）和漏报目标窗口。结果表明流式视频性能应被视为执行条件化的系统行为，而非单一分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式视频理解需要模型在证据到达时进行解读，但现有评估常只报告任务分数，却未指明证据何时变得有效、视觉历史如何维护、响应如何触发。因此相似的分数可能对应不同的工作负载、失败模式和操作行为，导致评估结果难以解释和比较。作者旨在厘清这些被忽视的因素，建立条件感知的评估范式。

Method: TRACE 框架由三部分组成：（1）时间审计的视觉任务，附带证据时机和指令相关的触发标注；（2）统一的因果 Core–Adapter 协议，用于控制信息可用性并记录实际的历史处理与响应事件；（3）多维度的报告机制，覆盖答案质量、及时性、响应选择行为、工作量、完成度和可靠性。实验在 517 个视频的 1240 条记录上，用 8 种配置评估 8 个公开模型/系统。

Result: 实验发现：近乎相同的 QA 准确率可能掩盖完成度、答案有效性和生成工作量上的显著差异；主动式性能可分解为响应质量、响应延迟、误报（目标窗口无效但后续窗口仍存在时发出响应）和漏报目标窗口四个维度。这表明传统单一分数评估无法反映系统实际行为差异。

Conclusion: 流式视频理解性能应被解释为执行条件化的系统行为，而非单一分数。作者公开了基准与代码（https://github.com/om-ai-lab/trace-bench），为后续研究者提供可复现的条件感知评估工具。

Abstract: Streaming video understanding requires models to interpret evidence as it arrives, yet current evaluations often report task scores without specifying when evidence becomes valid, how visual history is maintained, or how responses are triggered. As a result, similar scores may correspond to different workloads, failure modes, and operational behavior. We introduce TRACE (Temporal Audit and Condition-aware Evaluation), a condition-aware benchmark and evaluation framework that makes these factors explicit. TRACE combines temporally audited visual tasks with evidence timing and instruction-dependent trigger annotations, a unified causal Core--Adapter protocol that controls information availability while recording actual history processing and response events, and multidimensional reporting of answer quality, timeliness, response-selection behavior, workload, completion, and reliability. On 1,240 records from 517 videos, we evaluate eight publicly available models or systems in eight configurations. We find that nearly identical QA accuracy can mask substantial differences in completion, answer validity, and generation workload, while proactive performance separates into response quality, response delay, false alarms (responses emitted while no target window is currently valid and a later one remains), and missed target windows. These results show that streaming-video performance should be interpreted as execution-conditioned system behavior rather than a single score. Our benchmark and code can be accessed at \href{https://github.com/om-ai-lab/trace-bench}{https://github.com/om-ai-lab/trace-bench}.

</details>


### [25] [Words Speak Louder Than Order: A Behavioral Evaluation of Gemma 4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30716)
*Amanda Fitch*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究评估了Google预训练模型Gemma 4-e4b在接收相互矛盾文档时的决策机制，通过完全平衡的反向实验设计（13个项目、784次前向传播），发现来源框架（如官方指南vs新更新）对模型最终答案的影响远大于文档呈现顺序；模型表现出首因效应但强度差异高达5倍；结构整体重复（逐字模板）而非短触发短语驱动了这种位置偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 了解语言模型在处理冲突信息时如何决定优先级，具体探究来源框架（source framing）和阅读位置（presentation order）哪个对模型决策影响更大，以及位置偏差背后的具体机制。

Method: 在Google预训练的Gemma 4-e4b模型上进行靶向行为测试套件（n=13项，784次前向传播），采用完全平衡的实验设计（counterbalanced design），数学上隔离来源框架与阅读位置的独立效应，同时消除模型自然的词汇偏好偏差。在短篇、单轮上下文中设置十个测试条件。

Result: 1) 来源框架显著强于阅读位置：语义框架（如官方指南vs新更新）对最终答案的影响远超文档呈现顺序；2) 模型一致表现出首因效应（偏好先读到的文档），但该偏差强度受表面措辞影响波动至少5倍；3) 位置偏差由整体结构重复驱动——当两文档使用逐字模板结构完全相同时首因效应显著增加，而短促的重复触发短语（如"is [Answer]"）并非驱动因素，引入措辞变化可削弱该偏差。

Conclusion: 语言模型在冲突输入中的决策优先考虑来源的语义框架而非呈现顺序；首因效应确实存在但其强度高度依赖措辞；结构性的整体模板重复是位置偏差的关键放大器，而非表面的短句提示。这对提示工程和模型部署中信息来源的组织方式具有实践意义。

Abstract: When a language model receives two conflicting documents as input, how does it decide which one to prioritize? Does it rely on how the sources are framed or the presentation order of the documents? We evaluated this behavior on Google's pre-trained Gemma 4-e4b model across a targeted behavioral suite (n = 13 items, 784 forward passes in short, single-turn contexts) using a completely counterbalanced experimental design. This setup allowed us to mathematically isolate the specific effects of source framing and reading position, while ensuring the model's natural vocabulary biases were canceled out.
  Across ten test conditions, we discovered the following:
  1. Source framing heavily overpowers reading position. When directly competing, the semantic framing of a source (such as presenting it as an official guideline or a fresh update) had a significantly stronger impact on the model's final answer than the presentation order of the document.
  2. The model favors the first document it reads, but this bias is highly variable. While the model consistently demonstrated a primacy effect (preferring the first document presented), the actual strength of this bias fluctuated by at least a factor of 5 based solely on the surface wording.
  3. Overall structural repetition, not short copy-cues, drives positional bias. The model's preference for the first document is not a mechanical reaction to short, repetitive trigger phrases, such as "is [Answer]". However, the primacy effect does increase significantly when the two competing documents are structurally identical, using word-for-word verbatim templates. Introducing variation in the overall wording between the two sources reduces this positional bias.

</details>


### [26] [Beyond Mean Attention: Diversity-Aware, Layer-Wise Scoring for KV Cache Eviction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30738)
*Tianfang Xie,Wei Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究KV缓存淘汰中统一打分函数μ_i+λ₁σ_i+λ₂corr(i,S)，引入注意力分散度和与已选token的冗余惩罚（MMR式多样性）。研究发现单个全局多样性常数在16个LongBench数据集上（Mistral-7B，预算64）改善13/16（macro +1.1），且增益在预算32时保持、128时收窄；按数据集搜索发现仅passage retrieval存在显著的层间结构（中层符号翻转），在预算64下相对基线+9.6分、预算128下+13.2分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV缓存淘汰方法（如SnapKV、PyramidKV）仅以平均注意力打分，忽略了注意力分散度及token间冗余，作者希望统一这些信号并探究相关性-多样性平衡是否随深度变化。

Method: 提出统一打分μ_i+λ₁σ_i+λ₂corr(i,S)，其中λ₂<0时通过MMR式相关性惩罚增强多样性且无需额外前向传播。对比固定全局系数与三段式/二次深度分布曲线，仅在开发集上用sinh重参数化搜索深度分布超参数；通过逐数据集搜索+在保留测试集上重放所有接受的搜索状态来区分真实结构与调参噪声。

Result: 全局单一多样性常数在16个LongBench数据集上改善13个（macro +1.1），预算32时增益保持、128时收窄；逐数据集搜索发现多数数据集无层结构，但passage retrieval存在显著的中间层符号翻转，预算64下比基线+9.6、预算128下比全局常数+13.2（无需重调）；消融实验归因于冗余项。

Conclusion: 相关性-多样性平衡（MMR式冗余惩罚）能有效提升KV缓存淘汰性能，且该平衡的层间结构仅在特定任务（passage retrieval）上显著，提示深度依赖的多样性策略需按数据集任务定制而非全局统一。

Abstract: KV cache eviction methods such as SnapKV and PyramidKV rank tokens solely by mean attention over a small observation window. We study a unified score, $μ_i+λ_1σ_i+λ_2\mathrm{corr}(i,S)$, adding attention dispersion across window queries and redundancy relative to selected tokens. For $λ_2<0$, the score penalizes similarity to selected tokens as in maximal marginal relevance (MMR), without extra forward passes. To test whether this relevance-diversity balance should vary with depth, we compare fixed global coefficients with three-segment and quadratic profiles. Only these depth profiles are searched on a development split under a $\sinh$ reparameterization. On all 16 English LongBench datasets with Mistral-7B at a budget of 64 entries per layer, a single global diversification constant improves 13 of 16 datasets (macro +1.1); the gain holds at budget 32 and narrows at 128. Per-dataset search finds no detectable layer structure on most datasets; on passage retrieval it finds a large one: a mid-layer sign flip that rewards similarity and is worth +9.6 over the baseline at budget 64 and, without re-tuning, +13.2 over the global constant at budget 128. Ablations attribute the gain to the redundancy term; replaying every accepted search state on the held-out test set separates genuine structure from tuning noise.

</details>

Abstract: Multilingual text-vision embedding models are essential for cross-lingual image-text retrieval, but Southeast Asian languages remain poorly supported due to the region's linguistic diversity and limited data and computing resources. In this paper, we introduce SEA-CLIP-Tiny, a compact multilingual text-vision embedding model for Southeast Asia with fewer than 50M parameters. Our model adapts a CLIP-KD-style framework to Southeast Asian multilingual settings through regional data curation and multilingual teacher guidance. Experiments across seven Southeast Asian languages show that SEA-CLIP-Tiny achieves the strongest average retrieval performance among the evaluated student models, reaching 12.9%, 31.5%, and 42.2% at R@1, R@5, and R@10, respectively. Compared with MobileCLIP2, it improves average R@10 by 12.1 points while using 38.4% fewer parameters and lower measured CPU latency. These results highlight the importance of region-aware training for efficient multilingual text-vision models in Southeast Asia.

### [28] [Learning Natural Conversational Behavior in Tandem Speech-to-Speech Models with Randomized Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30773)
*Manato Yaguchi,Yotaro Kubo,Hikaru Asano,So Kuroki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出随机中间指导（randomized intermediate guidance）方法，用于训练串联式语音到语音（tandem speech-to-speech）架构KAME。该方法直接从对话语料库中衍生指导信号（目标响应提供信息性指导，随机采样响应提供潜在无关更新），避免了使用模拟器LLM生成指导所带来的大量数据准备开销。在合成对话上，该方法达到与LLM生成和基于相似度的基线相当的性能；在3.8k小时真实对话上训练后，相比合成数据KAME在轮转和自然度上表现出色，且保持优于Moshi的响应质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 串联式语音到语音架构将响应式语音前端与异步文本后端耦合，LLM后端在用户说话时提供候选响应作为前端指导。然而，普通对话录音只捕获最终响应，不包含后端在用户说话过程中提供的指导。使用模拟器LLM生成缺失指导需要在真实对话训练中引入大量数据准备开销。

Method: 提出随机中间指导方法：训练时直接从对话语料库中获取指导信号——目标响应提供富有信息量的指导，随机采样响应提供可能在话语期间不相关的更新。这种组合旨在教会前端有选择性地利用后端信息，而非模拟后端LLM行为。

Result: 在合成对话上，KAME使用该配方训练的响应质量与LLM生成及基于相似度的基线相当。在3.8k小时真实对话上训练后，相比合成数据KAME，轮转自然度和音频评测员自然度均有提升，同时保持优于Moshi的响应质量优势。

Conclusion: 随机指导为结合串联模型的响应质量优势与从真实语音中学习的自然对话行为提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Tandem speech-to-speech architectures couple a responsive speech frontend with an asynchronous text backend. In KAME, a large language model (LLM) serves as the backend, supplying candidate responses as guidance to the speech frontend while the user is still speaking. Ordinary conversation recordings capture the eventual response but not the guidance the backend would supply during the user's utterance. Generating the missing guidance with a simulator LLM adds substantial data-preparation overhead when training on real conversations. We propose randomized intermediate guidance, which derives guidance directly from the conversation corpus rather than simulating backend LLM behavior. During training, target responses provide informative guidance, while randomly sampled responses provide potentially irrelevant updates during the utterance. This combination aims to teach the frontend to use backend information selectively. On synthetic dialogues, KAME trained with this recipe achieves response quality comparable to that of the LLM-generated and similarity-based baselines. Training on 3.8k hours of real conversations improves smooth turn-taking and audio-judge naturalness over synthetic-data KAME while retaining a response-quality advantage over Moshi. These results show that randomized guidance offers a practical route to combining the response-quality benefits of tandem models with natural conversational behavior learned from real speech.

</details>


### [29] [Understanding the Role of Prompt Template in Knowledge Distillation for Safety Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30802)
*Anjila Budathoki,Manish Dhakal,Benjamin M. Ampel,Yi Ding*

TL;DR: 本文研究了知识蒸馏（KD）过程中提示模板选择对学生模型安全对齐的影响。研究发现，使用聊天模板（chat template）进行蒸馏会使模型对有害查询更顺从，导致安全对齐显著退化，而使用非聊天模板（non-chat template）则能更好地保留学生模型原有的内部表征和安全对齐能力。该发现在LLaMA、Gemma和Qwen三个模型家族及多个安全基准上均得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明监督微调（SFT）阶段的提示模板选择对后续安全对齐鲁棒性有显著影响，但知识蒸馏（KD）从教师到学生过程中模板选择的影响尚未被探索。作者旨在填补这一研究空白，分析不同模板配置如何影响学生模型预存在的安全对齐。

Method: 作者对三个模型家族（LLaMA、Gemma、Qwen）的指令微调模型进行知识蒸馏实验，分别使用聊天模板和非聊天模板进行蒸馏，然后在多个安全基准上评估学生模型的安全性。同时通过表征分析对比不同模板蒸馏下的内部表征变化。

Result: 实验发现：(1) 使用聊天模板蒸馏会显著降低学生模型的安全对齐，使其对有害查询更顺从；(2) 非聊天模板蒸馏能更好地保留学生模型原有的内部表征，而聊天模板蒸馏会引发更大的表征偏移；(3) 这些现象在LLaMA、Gemma和Qwen三个模型家族及多个安全基准上均保持一致。

Conclusion: 知识蒸馏中的提示模板选择对学生模型的安全对齐具有重要影响。使用非聊天模板进行蒸馏可以有效保留学生模型已有的安全对齐能力，而聊天模板会放大模型对有害内容的顺应性。该研究为实践中安全的知识蒸馏流程提供了重要的指导建议。

Abstract: Prior research has demonstrated that the choice of prompt template during Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) significantly impacts the robustness of safety alignment afterwards. However, the influence of template selection during Knowledge Distillation (KD) from teacher to student remains largely unexplored. Thus, we fill this gap by analyzing how different template configurations influence the pre-existing safety alignment of the student. We observe a significant degradation of safety alignment present in the aligned base instruct-tuned model. Specifically, we find that utilizing chat templates renders the model more compliant with harmful queries compared to a non-chat template. These findings are consistent across three models: LLaMA, Gemma and Qwen model families and are evaluated across multiple safety benchmarks. We further show that using a non-chat template during distillation better preserves the base student's internal representations, while chat template distillation induces a larger representational shift. Code: https://github.com/anjilab/role-of-prompt-template-in-kd

</details>


### [30] [I-Parakeet: Integer-Only Conformer ASR on Mobile NPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30846)
*Taichi Nishimura*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 I-Parakeet，一个完全整数化的 NVIDIA Parakeet-CTC (0.6B) 部署方案，在智能手机 NPU 上无需任何浮点算子或 CPU 回退即可运行，实现 4.97% WER 和 0.048 实时因子（较 CPU 快 7.5x）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代 Conformer 类 ASR 模型因体积庞大难以部署到边缘设备，且已有的量化模型在数值敏感运算上仍会回退到浮点，无法充分利用移动 NPU 等整数加速器。

Method: 三方面贡献：(1) 推导 Conformer 核心相对位置自注意力的整数公式，将不同量化尺度的两个分数分支及相对偏移融合为纯整数运算；(2) 提出 minimax 优化的 Swish 近似以最小化 Swish 输出的最大误差；(3) 逐层激活范围分析得出两个针对性改进——BatchNorm 输出采用 INT16 网格、对重尾的编码器前激活采用百分位校准。

Result: I-Parakeet 在 LibriSpeech test-other 上达到 4.97% WER，在 Qualcomm NPU 上以 0.048 的实时因子运行，比 CPU 基线快 7.5 倍。

Conclusion: 通过纯整数化自注意力、Swish 近似以及针对性的激活量化和校准方案，成功消除了所有浮点回退，使大规模 Conformer ASR 模型能在移动 NPU 上高效、端到端地整数运行。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose I-Parakeet, an integer-only implementation of NVIDIA's Parakeet-CTC (0.6B parameters) that runs on a smartphone NPU without any floating-point operator or CPU fallback. Modern Conformer ASR models are hard to deploy on edge devices because of their size, and quantized models still fall back to floating point for numerically sensitive operations. This prevents them from fully exploiting integer accelerators such as mobile NPUs. To achieve this, our contributions are threefold. First, we derive an integer formulation of the relative-positional self-attention at the core of the Conformer. We fuse its two score branches with different quantization scales and the relative shift into integer-only operations. Second, we introduce a minimax-optimized Swish approximation that minimizes the maximum error of the Swish output. Third, a layer-wise range analysis of activations yields two targeted remedies: an INT16 grid for the BatchNorm output and percentile calibration for the heavy-tailed pre-encoder activations. I-Parakeet achieves 4.97% WER on LibriSpeech test-other, running on a Qualcomm NPU at a real-time factor of 0.048, 7.5x faster than a CPU baseline.

</details>


### [31] [Enhancing Assessment of Self-Consistency in LLM Explanations using Perturbation Strength](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30849)
*Phuong Q. Le,Kemal Kurniawan,Jey Han Lau*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于LLM-as-a-judge的统一扰动强度度量方法，在输入扰动与思维链（CoT）扰动之间实现可比的强度控制，进而公平评估不同LLM生成解释的自洽性。实验表明该度量优于嵌入和基于概率的方法，且输入扰动对LLM的影响强于CoT扰动，说明自洽性判断仅在相同扰动类型内才具有公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究虽通过表面级扰动方法考察LLM生成解释的自洽性，但扰动强度未被显式测量与控制，导致不同扰动类型（输入扰动 vs CoT扰动）之间的比较缺乏公平基准。作者旨在建立统一、可量化的扰动强度度量，以支撑跨扰动类型、跨模型的公平自洽性评估。

Method: 提出LLM-as-a-judge方法，利用大语言模型作为裁判，对输入扰动和CoT扰动进行统一的扰动强度测量；在此基础上，在受控强度条件下评估多个LLM生成解释的自洽性，并与嵌入基（embedding-based）和概率基（probability-based）的强度度量进行对比验证。

Result: 实验结果显示，所提出的基于LLM的扰动强度度量优于嵌入基和概率基方法；同时发现输入扰动对LLM的影响普遍强于CoT扰动。

Conclusion: 研究指出，跨扰动类型进行模型自洽性判断是不公平的，结论仅在同一扰动类型内成立；这提示未来自洽性评估应严格限定扰动类型并采用统一的强度控制。

Abstract: Prior work has examined the self-consistency of LLM-generated explanations using surface-level perturbation methods. However, the strength of these perturbations is not explicitly measured and controlled. In this work, we propose an LLM-as-a-judge approach to measure perturbation strength in a unified manner across input and CoT perturbations. We then evaluate the self-consistency in explanations generated from various LLMs under controlled strength conditions, ensuring a fair comparison across perturbation types. Experiments show that our proposed LLM-based perturbation strength measure outperforms other embedding- and probability-based approaches and that input perturbations generally affect LLMs more strongly than CoT perturbations. Our work suggests that judgments about a model's self-consistency is fair only within the same perturbation type.

</details>


### [32] [Persistent Negatives for Adversarial Black-Box On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30864)
*Haixu Ma,Saad Lahrichi,Weiwei Li,Kevin Han,Weiqiang Wu,Peggy Yang,Dongzhuo Li,Ruiyi Li,Serena Li,Gedi Zhou,Mingze Gao,Abhishek Kumar,Xiangjun Fan,Lizhu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出持久负样本对抗蒸馏（persistent-negative adversarial distillation），一种用于黑箱在线策略蒸馏（OPD）的新方法。该方法通过维护一个包含历史教师-学生对比样本的实时池（live-pool），解决对抗蒸馏中由于判别器负样本分布随策略更新而漂移的"移动目标"问题。在等量判别器计算资源下，该方法相比现有方法在多个学生家族、评审模型和聊天基准上均取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在黑箱在线策略蒸馏中，教师仅提供采样响应而不提供token概率，对抗蒸馏通过学习区分教师与学生响应的判别器来提供策略奖励。但传统方法每次从最新学生策略采样负样本，导致判别器所学习的奖励函数依赖于一个随策略更新不断变化的负样本分布（即"移动目标"问题），这会影响奖励估计的稳定性与准确性。

Method: 提出持久负样本对抗蒸馏：在每次判别器训练批次中用一定比例的历史、提示匹配的教师-学生对比样本替换最新采样的负样本。历史样本用于训练判别器（与最新样本共享相同的判别器计算量），而GRPO继续使用最新的学生响应保持在线策略更新。理论上，作者推导了贝叶斯最优奖励为教师与负样本的对数密度比，并在显式假设下证明持久负样本能锚定判别器并降低奖励估计的均方误差。

Result: 在两种学生家族、三种评审模型和四个被评审的聊天基准上，持久负样本对抗蒸馏在同等判别器计算条件下相比现有方法一致地提升了性能。此外，该方法产生了更平滑的新策略判别器轨迹，低于随机水平（below-chance）的下降更少。

Conclusion: 判别器的负样本分布是黑箱在线策略蒸馏中的一个重要设计维度，持久负样本机制通过稳定判别器训练有效缓解了移动目标问题，为后续研究提供了新的优化方向。

Abstract: Black-box On-Policy Distillation (OPD) seeks to improve a student from its own generations when the teacher provides sampled responses but not token probabilities. Adversarial distillation offers one route: it learns a discriminator over prompt-matched teacher and student responses and uses its score as the policy reward. However, sampling discriminator negatives from the latest student at each step couples the learned reward to a negative distribution that changes after every policy update. We address this moving-target problem with persistent-negative adversarial distillation, a live-pool method that replaces a fraction of each discriminator batch with historical, prompt-matched teacher--student comparisons. Under matched discriminator compute, historical comparisons train the discriminator, while GRPO remains on-policy with fresh student responses. Our analysis identifies the Bayes-optimal reward as a teacher-to-negative log-density ratio and, under explicit assumptions, shows how persistent negatives anchor the discriminator and reduce reward-estimation MSE relative to fresh-negative training. Across two student families, three judges, and four judged-chat benchmarks, persistent-negative adversarial distillation consistently improves performance over current methods at matched discriminator compute. It also yields smoother fresh-policy discriminator trajectories, with fewer below-chance dips. These findings identify the discriminator's negative distribution as an important design axis in black-box on-policy distillation.

</details>

Abstract: Difference-in-differences (DID) studies are widely used to evaluate climate policy, but assessing the evidence supporting their identification assumptions remains challenging. We introduce ARGUS, a structured language-model pipeline that audits reported evidence against an eleven-dimension assumption-implication-evidence rubric and abstains when relevant evidence cannot be retrieved. We evaluate ARGUS using injected flaws, economics papers, and a small pilot with reconciled labels. On the 11-flaw benchmark, ARGUS detects 73% of planted flaws, compared with 18% for a keyword-based pipeline. Across 26 economics papers, ARGUS abstains on about 40% of paper-dimension assessments for lack of retrievable evidence. In a five-paper pilot with labels reconciled by two annotators, it assigns a higher risk level than the labels on 25 of the 33 assessments it completes. A rule fixed before the labels arrived removes most of this in-sample; weighted agreement stays low. ARGUS provides evidence-linked risk reports that localize potential weaknesses for expert review, without adjudicating causal claims. Code and data: https://github.com/yonghongzhang-io/ARGUS

</details>

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to assess long-form medical videos, but their effectiveness may depend on whether transcripts are provided in full or compressed through summarization, retrieval, or claim screening. This study examines how such transcript compression affects LLM-based veracity classification of Japanese medical YouTube videos. We compare four transcript input designs: full transcripts, LLM-generated summaries, RAPTOR-based retrievalaugmented generation (RAG), and Screening, which extracts candidate medical and health-related sentences. Using 74 long-form videos labeled as Real or Fake, we evaluate classification performance and analyze linguistic changes using J-LIWC, hedge expressions, and institutional or technical terms. The full-transcript Baseline achieved the best performance, whereas all compressed inputs increased false negatives, meaning that Fake videos were more likely to be misclassified as Real. Summary caused the largest performance drop, while Screening performed best among the compressed inputs but still omitted many medically relevant sentences. Linguistic analyses showed that these errors were not explained by a simple increase in certainty. Instead, Summary reduced affective, social, temporal, cognitive, and conversational cues, while Summary and RAG made institutional and technical terms more salient. These findings suggest that transcript compression can represent Fake videos as more coherent and authoritative inputs, thereby weakening cues needed for misinformation detection

</details>
